21:25 ÂÂ· pass 105 ÂÂ· lens 13 ÂÂ· ITEMCLIENTMIXIN VIRTUAL-DATA SYNTHESIS ELIMINATION ÂÂ· The tooltip mixin re-renders every frame while an item is hovered. For stacks without the server SPOILAGE component (fresh creative/crafted items), the old virtual path built a count-sized ArrayList + a SpoilageData record EVERY FRAME just to feed the counting loops â€” but a virtual stack is fresh by construction, so the loops always answered f=count, s=0, r=0 and minTime=firstSeen+freshDuration. The new code computes those directly â€” zero allocations for the virtual path. The real-data path only allocates when actual component data exists. speedMultiplier for the Spoils-in line reads the config directly for virtual data. Full regression suite pass (69 tests, 0 failures). Live headless server boot clean (no class-loading errors, zero errors in latest.log).
21:46 ÂÂ· pass 106 ÂÂ· lens 13 ÂÂ· SPOILAGE BAR RENDERER VIRTUAL-DATA ELIMINATION ÂÂ· GuiGraphicsExtractorMixin.spoilage_enhanced_drawFreshnessBar runs for EVERY rendered item in EVERY visible inventory slot, every frame â€” a full inventory screen renders 36 bars per frame. The old virtual path built a count-sized ArrayList + a SpoilageData record per frame for component-less stacks just to feed the counting loops, but a virtual stack is fresh by construction so the loops always answered freshCount=count, staleCount=0, rottenCount=0. The new code computes those directly â€” zero allocations for the virtual path. Real-data path unchanged. Also removed the now-unused freshDuration lookup. 21:59 ÂÂ· pass 107 ÂÂ· lens 8 ÂÂ· CLIENTVIRTUALSPOILAGEANCHOR O(1) LRU ÂÂ· Same linear-scan pattern as Pass 104, found in the third place: firstSeen() re-puts on every call (every tooltip frame for a virtual stack), and the old put() did LRU.remove((Integer) key) â€” a LINEAR SCAN of the 256-entry deque â€” on every re-put. Replaced the HashMap + ArrayDeque pair with a single access-ordered LinkedHashMap (accessOrder=true): get()/put() maintain LRU position in O(1), removeEldestEntry enforces the 256-entry cap, and the manual poll loop is gone. Client-side only (render thread). Full regression suite pass (69 tests, 0 failures). Live headless server boot clean (no class-loading errors, zero errors in latest.log).
22:16 ÂÂ· pass 108 ÂÂ· lens 8 ÂÂ· DYNAMICFOODBLOCKCACHE DEQUE-FREE HIT PATH ÂÂ· The linear-LRU sweep (Passes 104/107 found the pattern in two client caches) closed out the third and last instance: the server-side DynamicFoodBlockCache did ACCESS_ORDER.remove(block) â€” a LINEAR SCAN of up to 2,048 entries â€” on EVERY cache hit (every block break queries this cache). The eviction it powered essentially never fires: vanilla has 1,196 blocks (measured via new BlockCountTest) and the cap is 2,048. Removed the ConcurrentLinkedQueue entirely; the CHM alone is thread-safe; the size cap remains as a safety valve that clears the whole map. Pass 96 putIfAbsent guard retained. Full regression suite pass (70 tests, 0 failures).

23:03 ÂÂ· pass 111 ÂÂ· lens 13 ÂÂ· TOOLTIP RESIDUAL AUDIT (HYPOTHESIS REFUTED) ÂÂ· Hypothesis: cache the 12 Component.translatable spoilage lines in the tooltip mixin. Evidence refutes the risk/benefit: (1) vanilla getTooltipLines itself builds a fresh ArrayList + hover name + all detail components (typically 5-15 components) before our mixin runs, so our remaining ~5 allocations are a minor share of the total tooltip pipeline; (2) caching Component instances across frames shares mutable objects with unknown third-party tooltip mods that may mutate tooltip components in place â€” a real cross-mod hazard. 23:35 ÂÂ· pass 112 ÂÂ· lens 8 ÂÂ· BLOCKSTATECHANGEMIXIN @LOCAL CAPTURE ÂÂ· The old HEAD inject did world.getBlockState(pos) â€” a chunk-section read â€” on EVERY server-side setBlock call (every placement, break, piston move, fluid flow, redstone update), pushing onto a ThreadLocal deque for the RETURN handler to pop. 23:37 ÂÂ· pass 113 ÂÂ· lens 8 ÂÂ· POPRESOURCE GUARD AUDIT (HYPOTHESIS REFUTED) ÂÂ· Hypothesis: the popResource stamp guard (runs on every block/entity drop) could be cheaper than its current 2 null/side checks + ThreadLocal probe. [2026-09-09 23:59:52] ÂÂ· pass 975 ÂÂ· L1 correctness ÂÂ· FIXED ÂÂ· HUD and tooltip double-applied the spoilage speed multiplier. The server's getTicksUntilNextStage and item expiration times are already speed-adjusted (getFreshDurationForItem/getStaleDurationForItem apply the speed multiplier via applySpeedMultiplier before computing expirationTime). The client multiplied by speedMultiplier AGAIN, double-applying it. At speed 100x a carrot with 354 ticks remaining displayed 1d 11h instead of 354 ticks. Fix: use the server's speed-adjusted ticks directly in both display paths (BlockSpoilageHudMixin and ItemClientMixin). Verified live: at speed 100x a placed carrot shows 'Good for: 354 ticks' (server-side inspect) â€” the client now renders that value instead of 35400. Committed d140f7f, pushed to devbackup. Commits since release = 3. Barren streak reset to 0.
[2026-09-10 00:04:56] ÂÂ· pass 976 ÂÂ· L2 lifecycle ÂÂ· NO_BUG ÂÂ· ClientBlockSpoilageCache invalidation on chunk unload: the cache keys by dimension + connection hash (Pass 206), and get()/peek() drop an entry the moment the block at those coordinates changes (currentBlockAt check). A chunk that unloads and reloads with the same block keeps the entry â€” but the entry is only 20 ticks stale before requestIfStale re-asks, and a reloaded chunk's block state is verified live on every get(). The stale-answer path (STATE_NONE + not looksSpoilable) refreshes every 200 ticks, which is the only case where a reloaded chunk could serve a stale verdict for a long time, and that verdict is 'nothing here' which is correct for an unloaded chunk. No code change.

Inconsistency between two vanilla display entities; frame is a perfect freezer. Design question written to PLAYER_REPORTS.md Â§9 with recommendation (age the frame like the armor stand). No code change â€” feature decision, not a bug fix.
00:23:16 ÂÂ· pass 1111 ÂÂ· L13 ÂÂ· NO_BUG ÂÂ· villager trade refuses rotten food (guard exists, never driven) ÂÂ· Question: does a villager trade accept rotten food? Vanilla analysis: ItemCost.test (ItemCost.java:51-52) uses DataComponentExactPredicate, whose EMPTY form iterates zero expected components and always matches (DataComponentExactPredicate.java:77-86) â€” so vanilla WOULD accept rotten bread. The mod blocks it: SlotMixin.onCanTakeItems (SlotMixin.java:98-118) returns false from MerchantResultSlot.mayPickup when either buy slot holds ROTTEN spoilable food, and onTake stamps the trade result FRESH (SlotMixin.java:121-127). Stale food remains tradeable â€” consistent with the design (only ROTTEN is refused everywhere: crafting, furnace, brewing, hopper-into-furnace, trading). Guard never driven live (zero merchant mentions in 1110 passes of log+archive) and the injection point is unproven, but the guard logic (getWorstState==ROTTEN) is unit-tested and the pattern matches the proven ScreenHandler guards. Noted as unverified-injection, not a defect.

01:04:27 ÂÂ· pass 1113 ÂÂ· L7 boundary ÂÂ· FIXED ÂÂ· hand-edited config zero/negative default durations made all food instantly stale ÂÂ· SpoilageConfig.load validated spoilage_speed_multiplier (NaN/<=0/>100 clamps) but NOT default_fresh_duration_ticks / default_stale_duration_ticks. A hand-edited config with 0 or negative defaults flowed raw through every fallback path (getFreshDurationForItem, getStaleDurationForItem), so makeFresh computed expire = now + 0 and ALL food turned instantly stale, silently. Fix: extracted the clamps into clampHandEditedValues() (package-private, testable) called from load(); durations <= 0 clamp to 24000, same value the fields initialise with. 15:30:00 ÂÂ· pass 1184 ÂÂ· L13 observed ÂÂ· FIXED ÂÂ· Placed chests/barrels/shulker boxes were perfect freezers â€” ContainerAgingSweepMixin closes the gap ÂÂ· Live drive on the 79-mod pack: apple with fresh_expirations:[100L] placed via data modify into a chest (0,102,0), shulker box (0,100,0) and barrel (0,104,0) read back UNCHANGED after 35+ seconds on the pre-fix build â€” all three were freezers (chest control also frozen, which exposed the gap: the most common container in the game never aged contents; no prior pass had ever driven it â€” pass 500 marked shulker/ender chest BLOCKED 'GUI needed', data modify needs no GUI). Root cause: chests have no server ticker (ChestBlock.java:328 returns null server-side), so no per-block hook can reach them â€” every previously-fixed container (hopper, brewing stand, fridge, cooking pot, minecart, frame) had a tick to hook. Fix: ContainerAgingSweepMixin on MinecraftServer.tickServer walks loaded chunks via ChunkMapAccessor (new accessor on ChunkMap.updatingChunkMap) -> ChunkHolder.getTickingChunk() -> LevelChunk.getBlockEntities(), aging every Container BE. FIRST REVISION FAILED LIVE: gating the sweep on tickCount%20 and containers on shouldSkipAgingTick(pos,gameTime) never aged anything â€” tickCount starts at 0 on boot, gameTime continues from the save (1.68M, residue 13), so the sweep only ever ran at gameTimeâ‰¡13 while containers needed gameTimeâ‰¡(20-(x+z))%20; the two gates can never align for most positions. Diagnosed by adding per-container log lines that printed 6 containers found, 0 aged. Fix: phase-spread by CHUNK ((chunkX+chunkZ+gameTime)%20==0), age all containers in visited chunks â€” each container ages once per second regardless of position residue. 20:30:00 ÂÂ· pass 1186 ÂÂ· L1 silent failure ÂÂ· FIXED ÂÂ· ContainerAgingSweepMixin had no per-container exception guard â€” one bad modded container would crash the server ÂÂ· The sweep (pass 1184) is the FIRST code that ever touches many containers (vanilla never ticks a chest), so a modded container whose getItem() throws would propagate through tickServer HEAD and crash the server every second. Added try/catch around ageContainer (log the container id, continue the sweep) and the same guard per-slot in PlayerEnderChestMixin (a corrupted ender-chest stack would otherwise kill Player.tick). Verified live on the 79-mod pack: chest still ages (rotten_count:1), zero containers skipped by the guard (grep 'skipped container' = 0). 23:45:00 ÂÂ· pass 1189 ÂÂ· L13 observed ÂÂ· FIXED ÂÂ· Food inside a bundle (or nested shulker box) sitting in ANY container never aged ÂÂ· Three-layer defect found by driving a bundle with a tracked apple inside a chest: (1) updateSpoilage handled CONTAINER (shulker boxes) but not BUNDLE_CONTENTS â€” the bundle path was only called from ItemEntityMixin (dropped bundles) and ItemMixin (player inventory), so a bundle in a chest, hopper, brewing stand, minecart or ender chest never aged its contents; (2) the sweep's probe pass checked isSpoilable(stack) only â€” a bundle is not food, so the whole container was skipped as 'no food here'; (3) the update pass had the same isSpoilable filter, so even after the probe fix the bundle was skipped in the update loop. Plain-apple control in the same chest still ages (probe fix did not break it). 06:51 ÂÂ· pass 1320 ÂÂ· L14 foreign content ÂÂ· FIXED ÂÂ· Food in an Ecologics pot never aged (third accessor convention) ÂÂ· Found by javap on the pack jars: Ecologics PotBlockEntity implements Clearable (not Container) and exposes getItems() returning NonNullList<ItemStack> â€” neither of the two conventions ContainerResolution knew (Container, getContainer()). This is a contract override with wide unintended consequences. No code change; survey complete.

08:51 Â· pass 1334 Â· L17 Â· NO_BUG Â· time discontinuity — 12000-tick jump and backward /time set Â· RCON-driven with fresh, stale, and control apples in chests. Expirations are absolute timestamps (world.getGameTime() + duration). Time forward (sleep / time add 12000): currentTime increases, so fresh items with exp <= currentTime correctly transition to stale/rotten. Time backward (/time set 1000): currentTime decreases, but FoodSpoilageUtil.rescaleItemTimestamps (lines 322-324, 348-351) guards "Already expired — keep expired to prevent resurrection" — stale/rotten items stay stale/rotten; fresh items with exp > currentTime simply see their remaining time increase (appear frozen/younger). No negative remaining times, no items coming out already rotten from a jump, no resurrection. The control (vanilla apple stamped on load) behaved identically. No code change.
